{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01678aN6dBmRB48dUp13RPSi","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→```markdown\n     2→# ChirpPulse – Real-Time Social Sentiment Lake\n     3→\n     4→> A production-grade, **Rust-powered**, event-streaming platform that captures, enriches, and analyzes public social chatter _as it happens_.<br>\n     5→> Built for **sub-second** latency, **petabyte-scale** retention, and **data-scientist-friendly** extensibility.\n     6→\n     7→[![CI](https://github.com/your_org/chirppulse/actions/workflows/ci.yml/badge.svg)](https://github.com/your_org/chirppulse/actions)\n     8→[![Crates.io](https://img.shields.io/crates/v/chirppulse.svg)](https://crates.io/crates/chirppulse)\n     9→[![License: Apache-2.0](https://img.shields.io/badge/License-Apache%202.0-blue.svg)](LICENSE)\n    10→\n    11→---\n    12→\n    13→## ✨ Key Features\n    14→\n    15→| Domain                     | Feature                                                                                                    |\n    16→|----------------------------|-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|\n    17→| Stream Processing          | Zero-copy ingestion, back-pressure control, dynamic topic routing, _exactly-once_ semantics via **Kafka** |\n    18→| Enrichment & ETL           | Strategy-pattern plug-ins (language detection, toxicity scoring, graph extraction, location resolution)    |\n    19→| Storage & Lakehouse        | **Apache Iceberg** tables (schema evolution, partition pruning, ACID)                                      |\n    20→| Query & Analytics          | Real-time aggregations with **Materialize** connectors; batch OLAP via **DuckDB**                          |\n    21→| Observability & Resilience | Self-healing consumer groups, end-to-end tracing (OpenTelemetry), circuit-breaker patterns                 |\n    22→| Deployment                 | Kubernetes Helm charts, `docker-compose` sandbox, GitHub Actions CI/CD                                     |\n    23→\n    24→---\n    25→\n    26→## 🏗️ Architecture Overview\n    27→\n    28→```mermaid\n    29→graph LR\n    30→  subgraph Ingestion\n    31→    TW(Twitter Firehose) -->|JSON| G[Gateway Service]\n    32→    RD(Reddit Stream) --> G\n    33→    DC(Discord IRC) --> G\n    34→  end\n    35→\n    36→  G -->|Avro/Kafka| Q[Quality Control]\n    37→  Q -->|Kafka| E[Enrichment Workers]\n    38→  E -->|Parquet| L[Iceberg Data Lake]\n    39→  L -->|Materialized Views| D[(Dashboards)]\n    40→  L -->|gRPC| API{{/api/v1}}\n    41→```\n    42→\n    43→* **Gateway Service** – Authenticates providers, normalizes payloads, and writes events to Kafka topics.<br>\n    44→* **Quality Control** – Deduplicates, validates schema, and enforces PII removal.<br>\n    45→* **Enrichment Workers** – _Hot-swappable_ algorithms, loaded via the Strategy pattern.<br>\n    46→* **Data Lake** – Iceberg tables stored on S3/GCS; versioned with snapshots.<br>\n    47→* **Dashboards & API** – Serve insights to humans and automated agents.\n    48→\n    49→---\n    50→\n    51→## 🚀 Quick Start (Local Sandbox)\n    52→\n    53→> Requirements: `docker`, `docker-compose`, and ~6 GB free RAM.\n    54→\n    55→```bash\n    56→git clone https://github.com/your_org/chirppulse.git\n    57→cd chirppulse\n    58→\n    59→# Spin up Kafka, Schema Registry, Iceberg, and all microservices\n    60→make up      # or: docker-compose up --build\n    61→\n    62→# Tail logs\n    63→make logs\n    64→\n    65→# Shut everything down\n    66→make down\n    67→```\n    68→\n    69→Once containers are healthy, open:\n    70→\n    71→* Grafana – http://localhost:3000  (user/pass: admin/admin)\n    72→* API Playground – http://localhost:8080/docs\n    73→* Materialize SQL – `psql materialize://materialize@localhost:6875/materialize`\n    74→\n    75→---\n    76→\n    77→## 🦀 Rust Workspace Layout\n    78→\n    79→```\n    80→chirppulse/\n    81→├── crates/\n    82→│   ├── chirppulse-common/      # Models, error types, telemetry\n    83→│   ├── chirppulse-ingest/      # Gateway service\n    84→│   ├── chirppulse-qc/          # Quality-control service\n    85→│   ├── chirppulse-enrich/      # Enrichment workers\n    86→│   ├── chirppulse-lake/        # Iceberg abstraction layer\n    87→│   └── chirppulse-cli/         # CLI & admin tooling\n    88→└── services/                   # Docker images, configs\n    89→```\n    90→\n    91→---\n    92→\n    93→## 📝 Example: Writing a Custom Enrichment Strategy\n    94→\n    95→```rust\n    96→use async_trait::async_trait;\n    97→use chirppulse_common::{\n    98→    model::{Event, EnrichedEvent},\n    99→    strategy::{EnrichmentError, EnrichmentStrategy},\n   100→};\n   101→\n   102→/// A toy sentiment analyzer that counts positive words.\n   103→pub struct SimpleSentiment;\n   104→\n   105→#[async_trait]\n   106→impl EnrichmentStrategy for SimpleSentiment {\n   107→    async fn enrich(&self, ev: Event) -> Result<EnrichedEvent, EnrichmentError> {\n   108→        let positives = [\"great\", \"awesome\", \"🎉\", \"🚀\"];\n   109→        let score = ev\n   110→            .body\n   111→            .split_whitespace()\n   112→            .filter(|word| positives.contains(word))\n   113→            .count() as f32;\n   114→\n   115→        Ok(EnrichedEvent {\n   116→ SimpleSentiment {\n    async fn enrich(&self, ev: Event) -> Result<EnrichedEvent, EnrichmentError> {\n        let positives = [\"great\", \"awesome\", \"🎉\", \"🚀\"];\n        let score = ev\n            .body\n            .split_whitespace()\n            .filter(|word| positives.contains(word))\n            .count() as f32;\n\n        Ok(EnrichedEvent {\n            metadata: ev.metadata,\n Connect to the real-time event fire-hose.  \n     8→2. Configure an enrichment pipeline.  \n     9→3. Publish analytic results back to Kafka / Iceberg.  \n    10→4. Extend the platform with a **custom strategy** (plug-in) that can be hot-swapped in production.\n    11→\n    12→---\n    13→\n    14→## Quick-Start\n    15→\n    16→Add the SDK to your `Cargo.toml`:\n    17→\n    18→```toml\n    19→[dependencies]\n    20→chirp-pulse = { version = \"0.9\", features = [\"streams\", \"strategy\"] }\n    21→tokio        = { version = \"1\",   features = [\"full\"] }\n    22→```\n    23→\n    24→Consumers are fully `async` and integrate with the Tokio runtime.\n    25→\n    26→---\n    27→\n    28→## Data Model\n    29→\n    30→```rust\n    31→use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};\n    32→use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\n    33→use uuid::Uuid;\n    34→\n    35→/// A flattened representation of any micro-interaction captured by ChirpPulse.\n    36→///\n    37→/// The schema is forward-compatible and will evolve with optional fields.\n    38→/// Unknown fields must be gracefully ignored by downstream services.\n    39→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n    40→pub struct SocialEvent {\n    41→    /// Unique, idempotent identifier applied by the ingestion layer.\n    42→    pub id: Uuid,\n    43→\n    44→    /// Original platform: twitter, reddit, mastodon, etc.\n    45→    pub source: &'static str,\n    46→\n    47→    /// RFC-3339 timestamp of when the event was GENERATED on its native platform.\n    48→    pub occurred_at: DateTime<Utc>,\n    49→\n    50→    /// The raw, unmodified payload\n    51→    pub payload: serde_json::Value,\n    52→}\n    53→```\n    54→\n    55→---\n    56→\n    57→## Streaming the Fire-Hose\n    58→\n    59→```rust,no_run\n    60→use chirp_pulse::sdk::{\n    61→    firehose::{FireHoseConsumer, FireHoseConfig},\n    62→    prelude::*,\n    63→};\n    64→use tokio_stream::StreamExt;\n    65→\n    66→#[tokio::main]\n    67→async fn main() -> Result<(), chirp_pulse::Error> {\n    68→    // 1. Create a config with sensible defaults + overrides.\n    69→    let cfg = FireHoseConfig::builder()\n    70→        .brokers([\"kafka-broker-1:9092\", \"kafka-broker-2:9092\"])\n    71→        .group_id(\"brand-insights-team\")\n    72→        .topic(\"chirppulse.firehose.raw\")\n    73→        .auto_commit_interval_ms(5_000)\n    74→        .build()?;\n    75→\n    76→    // 2. Stand-up an asynchronous consumer.\n    77→    let mut consumer = FireHoseConsumer::connect(cfg).await?;\n    78→\n    79→    // 3. Consume indefinitely, applying back-pressure automatically.\n    80→    while let Some(event) = consumer.next().await {\n    81→        match event {\n    82→            Ok(record) => {\n    83→                println!(\n    84→                    \"Got event {} from {} at {}\",\n    85→                    record.value.id, record.value.source, record.value.occurred_at\n    86→                );\n    87→            }\n    88→            Err(e) => {\n    89→                // Non-fatal parsing errors (e.g. schema evolution) can be logged & skipped.\n    90→                eprintln!(\"⚠️  Undeserializable record skipped: {e:?}\");\n    91→            }\n    92→        }\n    93→    }\n    94→\n    95→    Ok(())\n    96→}\n    97→```\n    98→\n    99→Key characteristics:\n   100→* **At-least-once** delivery with idempotent IDs.\n   101→* **Offset management** handled by the SDK (exposed for advanced use-cases).\n   102→* Transparent **schema evolution** support via Serde’s non-strict deserialization.\n   103→\n   104→---\n   105→\n   106→## Enrichment Pipelines\n   107→\n   108→ChirpPulse ships with an opinionated but extensible ETL pipeline expressed as an\n   109→**observer pattern** backed by **Kafka Streams**.  \n   110→Below is a complete, runnable example that adds *language detection* and a custom\n   111→*toxicity score* while enforcing data-quality thresholds.\n   112→\n   113→```rust,no_run\n   114→use chirp_pulse::{\n   115→    sdk::{\n   116→        enrich::{Pipeline, Stage},\n   117→        firehose::{FireHoseConsumer, FireHoseConfig},\n   118→        producers::TopicProducer,\n   119→    },\n   120→    algorithms::{language::LangDetector, toxicity::ToxicityClassifier},\n   121→};\n   122→\n   123→#[tokio::main]\n   124→async fn main() -> Result<(), chirp_pulse::Error> {\n   125→    // Build ingestion side.\n   126→    let source_cfg = FireHoseConfig::builder()\n   127→        .topic(\"chirppulse.firehose.raw\")\n   128→        .group_id(\"enrichment-worker-a\")\n   129→        .build()?;\n   130→    let mut firehose = FireHoseConsumer::connect(source_cfg).await?;\n   131→\n   132→    // Build egress side.\n   133→    let sink = TopicProducer::builder()\n   134→        .topic(\"chirppulse.firehose.enriched\")\n   135→        .brokers([\"kafka-broker-1:9092\"])\n   136→        .build()?;\n   137→\n   138→    // Compose the pipeline.\n   139→    let pipeline = Pipeline::new()\n   140→        .with_stage(Stage::sync(\"language\", LangDetector::default()))\n   141→        .with_stage(Stage::async(\"toxicity\", ToxicityClassifier::new(0.85)?))\n   142→        .with_stage(Stage::sync(\"qc\", |mut event| {\n   143→            // Drop events that don't meet minimum quality scores.\n   144→            if let Some(tox) = event\n   145→                .metadata\n   146→                .get(\"toxicity\")\n   147→                .and_then(|v| v.as_f64())\n   148→            {\n   149→                if tox > 0.95 {\n   150→                    // Reject extreme toxicity\n   151→                    return None;\n   152→                }\n   153→            }\n   154→            Some(event)\n   155→        }));\n   156→\n   157→    // Run forever — pipeline maintains back-pressure internally.\n   158→    while let Some(event) = firehose.next().await.transpose()? {\n   159→        if let Some(enriched) = pipeline.process(event).await? {\n   160→            sink.send(&enriched).await?;\n   161→        }\n   162→    }\n   163→\n   164→    Ok(())\n   165→}\n   166→```\n   167→\n   168→### Building Blocks\n   169→\n   170→* `Stage::sync` – executes on the current task; suited for CPU-bound, fast ops.  \n   171→* `Stage::async` – spawns a bounded task-pool; suited for I/O or heavy compute.  \n   172→* `Pipeline::process` – short-circuit returns `None` to **filter** events.\n   173→\n   174→---\n   175→\n   176→## Implementing a Custom Strategy\n   177→\n   178→Analytical algorithms are loaded via an SPI-like **Strategy pattern** allowing them\n   179→to be upgraded *without cluster downtime*.\n   180→\n   181→```rust,no_run\n   182→use async_trait::async_trait;\n   183→use chirp_pulse::sdk::strategy::{AnalysisCtx, Strategy};\n   184→\n   185→/// Counts emoji usage in real-time.\n   186→pub struct EmojiRadar;\n   187→\n   188→#[async_trait]\n   189→impl Strategy for EmojiRadar {\n   190→    const NAME: &'static str = \"emoji-radar\";\n   191→    type Error = anyhow::Error;\n   192→\n   193→    async fn analyze(&self, ctx: &AnalysisCtx) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {\n   194→        let event = &ctx.event;\n   195→\n   196→        let text = event\n   197→            .payload\n   198→            .get(\"text\")\n   199→            .and_then(|v| v.as_str())\n   200→            .unwrap_or_default();\n   201→\n   202→        let emoji_count = text.chars().filter(|c| c.is_emoji_presentation()).count();\n   203→\n   204→        ctx.emit_metric(\"emoji_count\", emoji_count as f64).await?;\n   205→        Ok(())\n   206→    }\n   207→}\n   208→```\n   209→\n   210→To register your strategy at runtime:\n   211→\n   212→```rust\n   213→use chirp_pulse::sdk::strategy::{StrategyLoader, StrategyServer};\n   214→\n   215→#[tokio::main]\n   216→async fn main() -> Result<(), chirp_pulse::Error> {\n   217→    StrategyLoader::default()\n   218→        .register(Box::new(EmojiRadar))\n   219→        .load()?;\n   220→\n   221→    StrategyServer::bind(\"0.0.0.0:8080\").await?.run_forever().await\n   222→}\n   223→```\n   224→\n   225→---\n   226→\n   227→## REST / gRPC Gateways\n   228→\n   229→Every microservice exposes **OpenAPI v3** & **gRPC** reflection at `/api`.\n   230→Example (TypeScript generated client shown for completeness):\n   231→\n   232→```rust,ignore\n   233→// Equivalent Rust client is autogenerated via `tonic-build`.\n   234→import {FireHoseClient} from 'chirp-pulse/clients/firehose';\n   235→\n   236→const client = new FireHoseClient('grpc+tls://firehose.chirppulse.io');\n   237→\n   238→const stream = client.tail({topic: 'chirppulse.firehose.enriched'});\n   239→\n   240→for await (const event of stream) {\n   241→  console.log(event.id, event.source);\n   242→}\n   243→```\n   244→\n   245→---\n   246→\n   247→## Back-Pressure & Retry Semantics\n   248→\n   249→All producers and consumers expose a `pending()` gauge and `flush()` async call\n   250→allowing you to gracefully drain in-flight messages before shutdown signals.\n   251→\n   252→```rust,no_run\n   253→signal_hook::flag::register_conditional_default(\n   254→    signal_hook::consts::SIGTERM,\n   255→    || {\n   256→        println!(\"Shutdown requested. Flushing…\");\n   257→        producer.flush(); // async barrier\n   258→    },\n   259→);\n   260→```\n   261→\n   262→---\n   263→\n   264→## Error Handling\n   265→\n   266→* **Typed errors** (`chirp_pulse::Error`) cover connection failures, schema problems,\n   267→  and pipeline time-outs.\n   268→* Every fatal error is reported to **OpenTelemetry** traces *and* bubbled up.\n   269→\n   270→```rust\n   271→match consumer.next().await {\n   272→    Some(Err(chirp_pulse::Error::Deserializer(e))) => {\n   273→        metrics::increment_counter!(\"events_deser_failed\");\n   274→        // The SDK automatically commits the offset to avoid poison-pill loops.\n   275→    }\n   276→    Some(Err(e)) => return Err(e.into()),\n   277→    Some(Ok(event)) => { /* happy path */ }\n   278→    None => unreachable!(\"stream never ends\"),\n   279→}\n   280→```\n   281→\n   282→---\n   283→\n   284→## Contributing\n   285→\n   286→Pull-requests must pass:\n   287→\n   288→1. `cargo fmt --all -- --check`  \n   289→2. `cargo clippy --all-targets -- -D warnings`  \n   290→3. `cargo test` – including integration tests that target a **real** Kafka test-container.\n   291→\n   292→---\n   293→\n   294→© 2023–present **ChirpPulse**.  \n   295→Licensed under Apache-2.0.\n   296→```\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. Connect to the real-time event fire-hose.  \n2. Configure an enrichment pipeline.  \n3. Publish analytic results back to Kafka / Iceberg.  \n4. Extend the platform with a **custom strategy** (plug-in) that can be hot-swapped in production.\n\n---\n\n## Quick-Start\n\nAdd the SDK to your `Cargo.toml`:\n\n```toml\n[dependencies]\nchirp-pulse = { version = \"0.9\", features = [\"streams\", \"strategy\"] }\ntokio        = { version = \"1\",   features = [\"full\"] }\n```\n\nConsumers are fully `async` and integrate with the Tokio runtime.\n\n---\n\n## Data Model\n\n```rust\nuse chrono::{DateTime, Utc};\nuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\nuse uuid::Uuid;\n\n/// A flattened representation of any micro-interaction captured by ChirpPulse.\n///\n/// The schema is forward-compatible and will evolve with optional fields.\n/// Unknown fields must be gracefully ignored by downstream services.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct SocialEvent {\n    /// Unique, idempotent identifier applied by the ingestion layer.\n    pub id: Uuid,\n\n    /// Original platform: twitter, reddit, mastodon, etc.\n    pub source: &'static str,\n\n    /// RFC-3339 timestamp of when the event was GENERATED on its native platform.\n    pub occurred_at: DateTime<Utc>,\n\n    /// The raw, unmodified payload\n    pub payload: serde_json::Value,\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Streaming the Fire-Hose\n\n```rust,no_run\nuse chirp_pulse::sdk::{\n    firehose::{FireHoseConsumer, FireHoseConfig},\n    prelude::*,\n};\nuse tokio_stream::StreamExt;\n\n#[tokio::main]\nasync fn main() -> Result<(), chirp_pulse::Error> {\n    // 1. Create a config with sensible defaults + overrides.\n    let cfg = FireHoseConfig::builder()\n        .brokers([\"kafka-broker-1:9092\", \"kafka-broker-2:9092\"])\n        .group_id(\"brand-insights-team\")\n        .topic(\"chirppulse.firehose.raw\")\n        .auto_commit_interval_ms(5_000)\n        .build()?;\n\n    // 2. Stand-up an asynchronous consumer.\n    let mut consumer = FireHoseConsumer::connect(cfg).await?;\n\n    // 3. Consume indefinitely, applying back-pressure automatically.\n    while let Some(event) = consumer.next().await {\n        match event {\n            Ok(record) => {\n                println!(\n                    \"Got event {} from {} at {}\",\n                    record.value.id, record.value.source, record.value.occurred_at\n                );\n            }\n            Err(e) => {\n                // Non-fatal parsing errors (e.g. schema evolution) can be logged & skipped.\n                eprintln!(\"⚠️  Undeserializable record skipped: {e:?}\");\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n```\n\nKey characteristics:\n* **At-least-once** delivery with idempotent IDs.\n* **Offset management** handled by the SDK (exposed for advanced use-cases).\n* Transparent **schema evolution** support via Serde’s non-strict deserialization.\n\n---\n\n## Enrichment Pipelines\n\nChirpPulse ships with an opinionated but extensible ETL pipeline expressed as an\n**observer pattern** backed by **Kafka Streams**.  \nBelow is a complete, runnable example that adds *language detection* and a custom\n*toxicity score* while enforcing data-quality thresholds.\n\n```rust,no_run\nuse chirp_pulse::{\n    sdk::{\n        enrich::{Pipeline, Stage},\n        firehose::{FireHoseConsumer, FireHoseConfig},\n        producers::TopicProducer,\n    },\n    algorithms::{language::LangDetector, toxicity::ToxicityClassifier},\n};\n\n#[tokio::main]\nasync fn main() -> Result<(), chirp_pulse::Error> {\n    // Build ingestion side.\n    let source_cfg = FireHoseConfig::builder()\n        .topic(\"chirppulse.firehose.raw\")\n        .group_id(\"enrichment-worker-a\")\n        .build()?;\n    let mut firehose = FireHoseConsumer::connect(source_cfg).await?;\n\n    // Build egress side.\n    let sink = TopicProducer::builder()\n        .topic(\"chirppulse.firehose.enriched\")\n        .brokers([\"kafka-broker-1:9092\"])\n        .build()?;\n\n    // Compose the pipeline.\n    let pipeline = Pipeline::new()\n        .with_stage(Stage::sync(\"language\", LangDetector::default()))\n        .with_stage(Stage::async(\"toxicity\", ToxicityClassifier::new(0.85)?))\n        .with_stage(Stage::sync(\"qc\", |mut event| {\n            // Drop events that don't meet minimum quality scores.\n            if let Some(tox) = event\n                .metadata\n                .get(\"toxicity\")\n                .and_then(|v| v.as_f64())\n            {\n                if tox > 0.95 {\n                    // Reject extreme toxicity\n                    return None;\n                }\n            }\n            Some(event)\n        }));\n\n    // Run forever — pipeline maintains back-pressure internally.\n    while let Some(event) = firehose.next().await.transpose()? {\n        if let Some(enriched) = pipeline.process(event).await? {\n            sink.send(&enriched).await?;\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n```\n\n### Building Blocks\n\n* `Stage::sync` – executes on the current task; suited for CPU-bound, fast ops.  \n* `Stage::async` – spawns a bounded task-pool; suited for I/O or heavy compute.  \n* `Pipeline::process` – short-circuit returns `None` to **filter** events.\n\n---\n\n## Implementing a Custom Strategy\n\nAnalytical algorithms are loaded via an SPI-like **Strategy pattern** allowing them\nto be upgraded *without cluster downtime*.\n\n```rust,no_run\nuse async_trait::async_trait;\nuse chirp_pulse::sdk::strategy::{AnalysisCtx, Strategy};\n\n/// Counts emoji usage in real-time.\npub struct EmojiRadar;\n\n#[async_trait]\nimpl Strategy for EmojiRadar {\n    const NAME: &'static str = \"emoji-radar\";\n    type Error = anyhow::Error;\n\n    async fn analyze(&self, ctx: &AnalysisCtx) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {\n        let event = &ctx.event;\n\n        let text = event\n            .payload\n            .get(\"text\")\n            .and_then(|v| v.as_str())\n            .unwrap_or_default();\n\n        let emoji_count = text.chars().filter(|c| c.is_emoji_presentation()).count();\n\n        ctx.emit_metric(\"emoji_count\", emoji_count as f64).await?;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n```\n\nTo register your strategy at runtime:\n\n```rust\nuse chirp_pulse::sdk::strategy::{StrategyLoader, StrategyServer};\n\n#[tokio::main]\nasync fn main() -> Result<(), chirp_pulse::Error> {\n    StrategyLoader::default()\n        .register(Box::new(EmojiRadar))\n        .load()?;\n\n    StrategyServer::bind(\"0.0.0.0:8080\").await?.run_forever().await\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## REST / gRPC Gateways\n\nEvery microservice exposes **OpenAPI v3** & **gRPC** reflection at `/api`.\nExample (TypeScript generated client shown for completeness):\n\n```rust,ignore\n// Equivalent Rust client is autogenerated via `tonic-build`.\nimport {FireHoseClient} from 'chirp-pulse/clients/firehose';\n\nconst client = new FireHoseClient('grpc+tls://firehose.chirppulse.io');\n\nconst stream = client.tail({topic: 'chirppulse.firehose.enriched'});\n\nfor await (const event of stream) {\n  console.log(event.id, event.source);\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Back-Pressure & Retry Semantics\n\nAll producers and consumers expose a `pending()` gauge and `flush()` async call\nallowing you to gracefully drain in-flight messages before shutdown signals.\n\n```rust,no_run\nsignal_hook::flag::register_conditional_default(\n    signal_hook::consts::SIGTERM,\n    || {\n        println!(\"Shutdown requested. Flushing…\");\n        producer.flush(); // async barrier\n    },\n);\n```\n\n---\n\n## Error Handling\n\n* **Typed errors** (`chirp_pulse::Error`) cover connection failures, schema problems,\n  and pipeline time-outs.\n* Every fatal error is reported to **OpenTelemetry** traces *and* bubbled up.\n\n```rust\nmatch consumer.next().await {\n    Some(Err(chirp_pulse::Error::Deserializer(e))) => {\n        metrics::increment_counter!(\"events_deser_failed\");\n        // The SDK automatically commits the offset to avoid poison-pill loops.\n    }\n    Some(Err(e)) => return Err(e.into()),\n    Some(Ok(event)) => { /* happy path */ }\n    None => unreachable!(\"stream never ends\"),\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Contributing\n\nPull-requests must pass:\n\n1. `cargo fmt --all -- --check`  \n2. `cargo clippy --all-targets -- -D warnings`  \n3. `cargo test` – including integration tests that target a **real** Kafka test-container.\n\n---\n\n© 2023–present **ChirpPulse**.  \nLicensed under Apache-2.0.\n```","numLines":296,"startLine":1,"totalLines":296}}}

177→/// The filter gives an O(1) approximate membership test with controllable\n   178→/// false-positive rate.  We complement it with an exact check in the\n   179→/// concurrent `DashSet` to eliminate false positives at the cost of\n   180→/// additional in-RAM storage for the most recent `DEDUP_CAPACITY` IDs.\n   181→pub struct DuplicateDetector {\n   182→    // Fast, memory-efficient approximate set.\n   183→    bloom: RwLock<BloomFilter>,\n   184→    // Slow but exact set of recent IDs. Mark in Bloom filter and exact set.\n   211→                drop(bloom);\n   212→                {\n   213→                    let mut bloom = self.bloom.write().await;\n   214→                    bloom.insert(id);\n   215→                }\n   216→                self.exact.insert(id.clone());\n   217→                return Ok(());\n   218→            }\n   219→        }\n   220→        // Bloom filter says \"maybe\".         if self.exact.len() >= DEDUP_CAPACITY {\n   231→                if let Some(first) = self.exact.iter().next() {\n   232→                    self.exact.remove(first.key());\n   233→                }\n   234→            }\n   235→            self.exact.insert(id.clone());\n   236→            Ok(())\n   237→        }\n   238→    }\n   239→}\n   240→\n   241→//\n   242→// .create()?;\n   278→        Ok(Self { producer, topic })\n   279→    }\n   280→\n   281→    #[instrument(skip_all, fields(topic = self.topic))]\n   282→    async fn send(&self, event: &SocialEvent) -> Result<(), SinkError> {\n   283→        let payload = serde_json::to_vec(event)?;\n   284→        let record = FutureRecord::to(self.topic)\n   285→            .key(&event.event_id)\n   286→            .payload(&payload);\n   287→        let produce_future = self.producer.send(record, Duration::from_millis(PRODUCER_TIMEOUT_MS));\n   288→\n   289→        match produce_future.await {\n   290→            Ok(Ok(_)) => Ok(()),\n   291→            Ok(Err((e, _))) | Err((e, _)) => Err(SinkError::Produce(e.to_string())),\n   292→        }\n   293→    }\n   294→}\n   295→\n   296→//\n   297→// ───────────────────────────── Pipeline  ───────────────────────────────────────\n   298→//\n   299→\n   300→/// Thread-safe, hot-swapable registry of `QualityRule`s.\n   301→///\n   302→/// Data scientists can update the active ruleset at run-time by calling\n   303→/// [`RuleRegistry::replace_rules`] with a freshly built vector.\n   304→#[derive(Default)]\n   305→pub struct RuleRegistry {\n   306→    inner: RwLock<Vec<Arc<dyn QualityRule>>>,\n   307→}\n   308→\n   309→impl RuleRegistry {\n   310→    pub fn new(initial: Vec<Arc<dyn QualityRule>>) -> Self {\n   311→        Self {\n   312→ 322→\n   323→    /// Fetch a snapshot clone of the current rules.\n   324→    pub async fn snapshot(&self) -> Vec<Arc<dyn QualityRule>> {\n   325→        self.inner.read().await.clone()\n   326→    }\n   327→}\n   328→\n   329→/// The main orchestrator that wires sources, rules and sink.\n   330→pub struct QualityPipeline {\n   331→    rules: Arc<RuleRegistry>,\n   332→    sink: from `consumer` indefinitely until `shutdown` is called.\n   352→    #[instrument(skip_all, name = \"quality_pipeline\")]\n   353→    pub async fn run(&self, consumer: StreamConsumer) -> anyhow::Result<()> {\n   354→        info!(\"QC Pipeline starting\");\n   355→        // Subscribe to topic.\n   356→        consumer.subscribe(&[SOURCE_TOPIC])?;\n   357→\n   358→        // Stream loop with back-pressure.\n   359→        let mut stream = kafka_event_stream(&consumer);\n   360→\n   361→        loop {\n   362→            select! {\n   363→                cancelled = maybe_msg = stream.next() => {\n   368→                    match maybe_msg {\n   369→                        None => {\n   370→                            warn!(\"Event stream unexpectedly ended\");\n   371→                            break;\n   372→                        }\n   373→                        Some(Err(err)) => {\n   374→                            error!(error = %err, \"Event stream error\");\n   375→                            counter!(\"pipeline_errors_total\", 1, \"stage\" => \"source\");\n   376→                        }\n   377→                        Some(Ok(mut event)) => {\n   378→                            // Process one event\n   379→                            let start = SystemTime::now();\n   380→\n   381→                            match self.process_event(&mut event).await {\n   382→                                Ok(()) => {\n   383→                                    let latency = SystemTime::now()\n   384→                                        .duration_since(start)\n   385→                                        .unwrap_or_default()\n   386→                                        .as_millis() as f64;\n   387→                                    histogram!(\"pipeline_event_latency_ms\", latency);\n   388→                                }\n   389→                                Err(e) => {\n   390→                                    error!(error = %e, \"Processing error\");\n   391→                                    counter!(\"pipeline_errors_total\", 1, \"stage\" => \"processing\");\n   392→                                    // Push to DLQ\n   393→                                    if let Err(dlq_err) = self.sink_dead_letter(&event, &e).await {\n   394→                                        error!(error = %dlq_err, \"Failed to write to DLQ\");\n   395→                                        counter!(\"pipeline_errors_total\", 1, \"stage\" => \"dlq\");\n   396→                                    }\n   397→                                }\n   398→                            }\n   399→                        }\n   400→                    }\n   401→                }\n   402→            }\n   403→        }\n   404→\n   405→        info!(\"QC Pipeline stopped\");\n   406→        Ok(())\n   407→    }\n   408→\n   409→    #[instrument(skip_all)]\n   410→    async fn process_event(&self, event: &mut SocialEvent) -> Result<(), PipelineError> {\n   411→        let rules = self.rules.snapshot().await;\n   412→\n   413→        for rule in rules.iter() {\n   414→            // For every rule we catch its error separately to emit per-rule metrics.\n   415→            match rule.check(event).await {\n   416→                Ok(()) => increment_counter!(\"rule_pass_total\", \"rule\" => rule.name()),\n   417→                Err(err) => {\n   418→                    increment_counter!(\"rule_fail_total\", \"rule\" => rule.name());\n   419→                    return Err(PipelineError::Rule(err));\n   420→                }\n   421→            }\n   422→        }\n   423→\n   424→        // Forward to sink\n   425→        self.sink.send(event).await?;\n   426→        Ok(())\n   427→    }\n   428→\n Quality-Control & Enrichment stage for ChirpPulse’s real-time ETL\n//!\n//! This module owns the “QC/Enrich” micro-service that sits right after\n//! raw ingestion.  It validates structural and business constraints,\n//! removes duplicates in a partially ordered fashion, enriches events\n//! with lightweight metadata (e.g. language codes already discovered by a\n//! previous stage), and forwards the passing events to the next Kafka\n//! topic.\n//!\n//! The code showcases several architectural patterns used across\n//! ChirpPulse:\n//!   • Pipeline Pattern—each rule implements `QualityRule` and runs in\n//!     parallel over an async stream.\n//!   • Strategy Pattern—rules can be hot-swapped at run-time without\n//!     stopping the pipeline.\n//!   • Observer Pattern—metrics are emitted for every rule and error.\n//!\n//! The implementation is fully asynchronous (Tokio) and production-grade\n//! with backpressure, cancellation and self-healing retry Option<String>,\n    pub created_at: u64, // epoch millis\n}\n\n/// Error emitted by quality-control rules.\n#[derive(thiserror::Error, Debug)]\npub enum QualityError {\n    #[error(\"missing #[error(\"event already seen (duplicate)\")]\n    Duplicate,\n    #[error(\"internal rule error: {0}\")]\n    Internal(anyhow::Error),\n}\n\n/// Errors that invalidate producing to Kafka.\n/// These We complement it with an exact check in the\n/// concurrent `DashSet` to eliminate false positives at the cost of\n/// additional in-RAM storage for the most recent `DEDUP_CAPACITY` IDs.\npub struct DuplicateDetector {\n    // Fast, memory-efficient approximate set.\n    bloom: RwLock<BloomFilter>,\n    // Slow but exact set of recent IDs.  Evicted by size limits.\n    exact: DashSet<String>,\n}\n\nimpl DuplicateDetector {\n    pub fn new(capacity: usize, fp_rate: f64) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            bloom: RwLock::new(BloomFilter::with_rate(fp_rate, capacity)),\n            exact: DashSet::with_capacity(capacity),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n#[async_trait]\nimpl QualityRule for DuplicateDetector {\n    fn name(&self) -> &'static str {\n        \"duplicate_detector\"\n    }\n\n    async fn check(&self, event: &mut SocialEvent) -> Result<(), QualityError> {\n        let id = &event.event_id;\n\n        // First, try the Bloom filter (approximate).\n        {\n            let bloom = self.bloom.read().await;\n            if !bloom.contains(id) {\n                // Definitely not seen before.  Mark in Bloom filter and exact set.\n                drop(bloom);\n                {\n                    let mut bloom = self.bloom.write().await;\n                    bloom.insert(id);\n                }\n                self.exact.insert(id.clone());\n                return Ok(());\n            }\n        }\n        // Bloom filter says \"maybe\". Perform exact check.\n        if self.exact.contains(id) {\n            Err(QualityError::Duplicate)\n        } else {\n            // False positive, record now.\n            {\n                let mut bloom = self.bloom.write().await;\n                bloom.insert(id);\n            }\n            // Evict if over capacity.\n            if self.exact.len() >= DEDUP_CAPACITY {\n                if     }\n    }\n}\n\n//\n// ──────────────────────────── Event Source ─────────────────────────────────────\n//\n\n/// Convert a borrowed Kafka message to `SocialEvent`.\nfn deserialize_event(msg: Err(SinkError::Produce(e.to_string())),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n//\n// ───────────────────────────── Pipeline  ───────────────────────────────────────\n//\n\n/// Thread-safe, hot-swapable registry of self.inner.write().await;\n        *guard = rules;\n        info!(count = guard.len(), \"Replaced quality-ruleset\");\n    }\n\n    /// Fetch a snapshot clone of the current rules.\n    pub async fn rules: Arc<RuleRegistry>,\n    sink: Arc<KafkaJsonSink>,\n    cancel: Arc<RwLock<bool>>,\n}\n\nimpl QualityPipeline {\n    pub fn new(rules: Arc<RuleRegistry>, sink: Arc<KafkaJsonSink>) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            rules,\n            sink,\n            cancel: Arc::new(RwLock::new(false)),\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Request graceful shutdown.\n    pub async fn shutdown(&self) {\n        let mut guard = self.cancel.write().await;\n        *guard = true;\n    }\n\n    /// Consumes from `consumer` indefinitely until `shutdown` is called.\n    #[instrument(skip_all, name = \"quality_pipeline\")]\n    pub async fn run(&self, consumer: StreamConsumer) -> anyhow::Result<()> {\n        info!(\"QC Pipeline starting\");\n        // Subscribe to topic.\n        consumer.subscribe(&[SOURCE_TOPIC])?;\n\n        // Stream loop with back-pressure.\n        let mut stream = kafka_event_stream(&consumer);\n\n        loop {\n            select! {\n                cancelled = self.cancel.read(), if *cancelled => {\n                    info!(\"QC Pipeline received shutdown signal\");\n                    break;\n                }\n                maybe_msg = counter!(\"pipeline_errors_total\", 1, \"stage\" => \"source\");\n                        }\n                        Some(Ok(mut event)) => {\n                            // Process one event\n    }\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        info!(\"QC Pipeline stopped\");\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    #[instrument(skip_all)]\n    async fn process_event(&self, event: &mut SocialEvent) -> Result<(), PipelineError> {\n        let rules = self.rules.snapshot().await;\n\n        for rule in rules.iter() {\n            // For every rule we catch its error separately to emit per-rule metrics.\n            match rule.check(event).await {\n                Ok(()) => increment_counter!(\"rule_pass_total\", \"rule\" => rule.name()),\n                Err(err) => {\n                    increment_counter!(\"rule_fail_total\", \"rule\" => rule.name());\n                    return Err(PipelineError::Rule(err));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Forward to sink\n        self.sink.send(event).await?;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n .key(&event.event_id)\n            .payload(&serde_json::to_vec(&payload)?);\n\n        match dlq_sink\n            .producer\n            .send(record, Duration::from_millis(PRODUCER_TIMEOUT_MS))\n            .await\n        {\n            Ok(Ok(_)) => Ok(()),\n            Ok(Err((e, _))) | Err((e, _)) => Err(SinkError::Produce(e.to_string())),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n//\n// .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)\n            .unwrap_or_default()\n            .as_millis() as u64;\n        Self {\n            event,\n            error: error.to_string(),\n            failed_at,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n//\n// ──────────────────────────────── main  ────────────────────────────────────────\n//\n\n#[tokio::main]\nasync fn main() -> anyhow::Result<()> {\n    // Initialise tracing and 0f32;\n   157→        let mut hits = 0u32;\n   158→\n   159→        for token in text.split_whitespace() {\n   160→            if self.positives.iter().any(|p| token.eq_ignore_ascii_case(p)) {\n   161→                score += 1.0;\n   162→                hits += 1;\n   163→            } else if self.negatives.iter().any(|n| token.eq_ignore_ascii_case(n)) {\n   164→                score -= 1.0;\n   165→                hits += 1;\n   166→            }\n   167→        }\n   168→\n   169→        if hits == 0 {\n   170→            // Unknown sentiment\n   171→            return 243→            .cloned()\n   244→            .ok_or_else(|| ProcessorError::StrategyNotFound(name.into()))\n   245→    }\n   246→}\n   247→\n   248→///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n   249→// Kafka configuration helpers\n   250→///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n   251→\n   252→const INPUT_TOPIC: &str = \"raw_social_stream\";\n   253→const OUTPUT_TOPIC: &str = \"enriched_social_stream\";\n   254→const DLQ_TOPIC: &str = \"enrichment_dlq\";\n   255→const STRATEGY_CONTROL_TOPIC: &str = \"sentiment_strategy_ctrl\";\n   256→\n   257→fn kafka_config(brokers: &str, group_id: &str) -> ClientConfig {\n   258→    let mut cfg = ClientConfig::new();\n   259→    cfg.set(\"bootstrap.servers\", brokers)\n   260→        .set(\"enable.auto.commit\", \"false\")\n .set(\"message.timeout.ms\", \"5000\");\n   265→    cfg\n   266→}\n   267→\n   268→fn build_consumer(brokers: &str, group_id: &str) -> Result<StreamConsumer, KafkaError> {\n   269→ .create::<FutureProducer>()\n   279→}\n   280→\n   281→///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n   282→// Stream Processor\n   283→///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n   284→\n   285→pub struct StreamProcessor {\n   286→    registry: StrategyRegistry,\n   287→    consumer:              {\n   349→                            error!(\"processing error: {:?}\", err);\n   350→                        }\n   351→                    });\n   352→                }\n   353→ 360→\n   361→    async fn handle_message(\n   362→        msg: OwnedMessage,\n   363→        topic: &str,\n   364→        registry: StrategyRegistry,\n   365→        producer: FutureProducer,\n   366→        dlq_tx: mpsc::Sender<OwnedMessage>,\n   367→    ) -> Result<(), ProcessorError> {\n   368→        match topic {\n   369→            INPUT_TOPIC => {\n   370→                let payload =            producer\n   396→                    .send(\n   397→                        FutureRecord::to(OUTPUT_TOPIC)\n   398→                            .payload(&serialized)\n   399→ .map_err(|(e, _)| ProcessorError::Kafka(e))?;\n   404→\n   405→                // manual commit\n   406→                if let Err(e) = msg.commit(&producer.client(), topic '{}', ignoring\", topic);\n   424→            }\n   425→        }\n   426→        Ok(())\n   427→    }\n   428→}\n   429→\n This module demonstrates how data-scientists can plug-in new sentiment\n//! algorithms without taking the pipeline offline.  A registry keeps multiple\n//! strategies in memory and a lightweight control message on the\n//! `STRATEGY_CONTROL_TOPIC` lets operators promote a new default strategy\n//! instantly.  The processor consumes raw social messages, enriches them with\n//! sentiment, and republishes them to the lake in real-time.\n//!\n//! Production-ready aspects demonstrated here:\n//! • async Kafka consumer/producer (`rdkafka` + `tokio`)  \n//! • Strategy pattern with dynamic dispatch & hot-swap capabilities  \n//! • Robust error handling (`thiserror`) and observability (`tracing`)  \n//! • Graceful back-pressure and DLQ for poison messages  \n//!\n//! NOTE: In a real deployment this module would live in its own crate with the\n//! dependencies declared in `Cargo.toml`.  For brevity all source is presented\n//! in a single file.\n\nuse std::{\n    collections::HashMap,\n    sync::Arc,\n    time::{Duration, SystemTime},\n};\n\nuse async_trait::async_trait;\nuse     let mut score = 0f32;\n        let mut hits = 0u32;\n\n        for token in text.split_whitespace() {\n            if self.positives.iter().any(|p| token.eq_ignore_ascii_case(p)) {\n = self.default.read().await.clone();\n        self.get(&name)\n            .await\n            .unwrap_or_else(|_| Arc::new(NeutralStrategy))\n    }\n\n    /// Fetch a strategy by name.\n    pub async fn get(&self, name: &str) -> Result<Arc<dyn SentimentStrategy>, ProcessorError> {\n        self.strategies\n            .read()\n            .await\n            .get(name)\n            .cloned()\n            .ok_or_else(|| ProcessorError::StrategyNotFound(name.into()))\n    }\n}\n\n///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n// Kafka configuration helpers\n///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n\nconst INPUT_TOPIC: &str = \"raw_social_stream\";\nconst OUTPUT_TOPIC: &str = \"enriched_social_stream\";\nconst DLQ_TOPIC: &str = \"enrichment_dlq\";\nconst STRATEGY_CONTROL_TOPIC: &str = \"sentiment_strategy_ctrl\";\n\nfn kafka_config(brokers: &str, group_id: &str) -> ClientConfig {\n    let mut cfg = ClientConfig::new();\n    cfg.set(\"bootstrap.servers\", brokers)\n        .set(\"enable.auto.commit\", \"false\")\n        .set(\"auto.offset.reset\", \"earliest\")\n        .set(\"group.id\", group_id)\n        .set(\"fetch.message.max.bytes\", \"1048576\") // 1 MiB\n        .set(\"message.timeout.ms\", \"5000\");\n    cfg\n}\n\nfn build_consumer(brokers: &str, group_id: &str) -> Result<StreamConsumer, KafkaError> {\n    kafka_config(brokers, group_id)\n        .create::<StreamConsumer>()\n}\n\nfn build_producer(brokers: &str) -> Result<FutureProducer, KafkaError> {\n    ClientConfig::new()\n        .set(\"bootstrap.servers\", brokers)\n        .set(\"message.timeout.ms\", \"5000\")\n        .set(\"compression.type\", \"lz4\")\n        .create::<FutureProducer>()\n}\n\n///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n// Stream Processor\n///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n\npub struct StreamProcessor {\n    registry: StrategyRegistry,\n    consumer: StreamConsumer,\n    producer: FutureProducer,\n}\n\nimpl StreamProcessor {\n    pub fn new(\n        registry: StrategyRegistry,\n        consumer: StreamConsumer,\n        producer: FutureProducer,\n    ) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            registry,\n            consumer,\n            producer,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Run the event loop until process is terminated.\n    pub async fn run(mut self) -> Result<(), ProcessorError> {\n        // Subscribe to both the data topic and the control topic\n        self.consumer\n            .subscribe(&[INPUT_TOPIC, STRATEGY_CONTROL_TOPIC])?;\n\n        info!(\"Sentiment enrichment processor started, awaiting messages...\");\n        let (dlq_tx, mut dlq_rx) = mpsc::channel::<OwnedMessage>(1024);\n\n        // Spawn DLQ producer\n        let producer_clone = self.producer.clone();\n        tokio::spawn(async move {\n            while let Some(msg) = dlq_rx.recv().await {\n                let payload = msg.payload().unwrap_or_default();\n                if let Err(e) = producer_clone\n                    .send(\n                        FutureRecord::to(DLQ_TOPIC)\n                            .payload(payload)\n                            .key(msg.key().unwrap_or_default()),\n                        Duration::from_secs(0),\n                    )\n                    .await\n                {\n                    error!(\"failed to produce to DLQ: {}\", e.0);\n                }\n            }\n        });\n\n        let stream = self.consumer.stream();\n        tokio::pin!(stream);\n\n        while let Some(result) = stream.next().await {\n            match result {\n                Ok(msg) => {\n                    let topic = msg.topic().to_owned();\n                    let owned = msg.detach();\n\n                    let registry = self.registry.clone();\n                    let producer = self.producer.clone();\n                    let dlq_tx = dlq_tx.clone();\n\n                    tokio::spawn(async move {\n                        if let Err(err) =\n                            Self::handle_message(owned, &topic, registry, producer, dlq_tx).await\n                        {\n                            error!(\"processing error: {:?}\", err);\n                        }\n                    });\n                }\n                Err(e) => {\n                    warn!(\"Kafka error encountered: {}\", e);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    async fn handle_message(\n        msg: OwnedMessage,\n        topic: &str,\n        registry: StrategyRegistry,\n        producer: FutureProducer,\n        dlq_tx: mpsc::Sender<OwnedMessage>,\n    ) -> Result<(), ProcessorError> {\n        match topic {\n            INPUT_TOPIC => {\n                let payload = std::str::from_utf8(msg.payload().unwrap_or_default())\n                    .map_err(|e| ProcessorError::Other(e.to_string()))?;\n\n                let social: SocialMessage = match serde_json::from_str(payload) {\n                    Ok(v) => v,\n                    Err(e) => {\n                        warn!(\"invalid JSON payload, sending to DLQ: {}\", e);\n                        dlq_tx.send(msg).await?;\n                        return Ok(());\n                    }\n                };\n\n                let strategy = registry.default().await;\n                let sentiment = strategy.analyze(&social.text).await?;\n\n                let enriched = EnrichedSocialMessage {\n                    original: social,\n                    sentiment,\n                    strategy: strategy.name().to_owned(),\n                    enriched_at: SystemTime::now(),\n                };\n\n                let serialized = serde_json::to_vec(&enriched)?;\n\n                // produce to output topic\n                producer\n                    .send(\n                        FutureRecord::to(OUTPUT_TOPIC)\n                            .payload(&serialized)\n                            .key(&enriched.original.id),\n                        Duration::from_secs(0),\n                    )\n                    .await\n                    .map_err(|(e, _)| ProcessorError::Kafka(e))?;\n\n                // manual commit\n                if let Err(e) = msg.commit(&producer.client(), rdkafka::consumer::CommitMode::Async)\n                {\n                    warn!(\"commit failed: {}\", e);\n                }\n            }\n            STRATEGY_CONTROL_TOPIC => {\n                let cmd: StrategyControl =\n                    serde_json::from_slice(msg.payload().unwrap_or_default())?;\n\n                match cmd {\n                    StrategyControl::Promote { name } => {\n                        registry.promote_default(&name).await?;\n                        info!(\"Promoted strategy '{}' as new default\", &name);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            _ => {\n                warn!(\"message on unexpected topic '{}', ignoring\", topic);\n            }\n        }\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n\n///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n// Strategy Control Plane\n///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n#[serde(tag = \"type\", rename_all = \"snake_case\")]\npub enum StrategyControl {\n    Promote { name: String },\n}\n\n///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n// Embedded integration test (requires 'kafka-integration-test' feature flag)\n///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use tokio::time::timeout;\n\n    /// Simple smoke test ensuring lexicon logic works in isolation.\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_lexicon_strategy() {\n        let strat = LexiconStrategy::default();\n        let res = strat\n            .analyze(\"I love great, excellent code but hate bugs\")\n            .await\n            .unwrap();\n        assert!(res.polarity.abs() > 0.0);\n    }\n\n    /// Registry promotion logic.\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_registry_promote() {\n        let registry = StrategyRegistry::new();\n        registry.promote_default(\"neutral\").await.unwrap();\n        let default = registry.default().await;\n        assert_eq!(default.name(), \"neutral\");\n    }\n\n    /// Ensure invalid promotion yields error.\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_registry_promote_invalid() {\n        let registry = StrategyRegistry::new();\n        let err = registry.promote_default(\"non-existent\").await.unwrap_err();\n        matches!(err, ProcessorError::StrategyNotFound(_));\n    }\n\n    /// Strategy control serialization round-trip.\n    #[test]\n    fn test_strategy_control_ser() {\n        let msg = StrategyControl::Promote {\n            name: \"lexicon_v1\".into(),\n        };\n        let json = serde_json::to_string(&msg).unwrap();\n        let back: StrategyControl = serde_json::from_str(&json).unwrap();\n        match back {\n            StrategyControl::Promote { name } => assert_eq!(name, \"lexicon_v1\"),\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Integration with Kafka omitted in unit tests; would be covered in system\n    // tests using testcontainers or tracing_subscriber::fmt::init();\n\n    let brokers = std::env::var(\"KAFKA_BROKERS\").unwrap_or_else(|_| \"localhost:9092\".to_string());\n    let registry = StrategyRegistry::new();\n    let consumer = build_consumer(&brokers, \"chirppulse-sentiment\")?;\n    let producer = build_producer(&brokers)?;\n\n    let processor = StreamProcessor::new(registry, consumer, producer);\n    processor.run().await?;\n    Ok(())\n}\n```","numLines":512,"startLine":1,"totalLines":512}}}

Real-time enrichment and quality control pipeline.\n     4→//!\n     5→//! This module implements a configurable, pluggable enrichment pipeline that\n     6→//! continuously consumes `SocialEvent` items from a Kafka topic (or any\n     7→//! compatible [`Stream`]) and publishes the transformed events to downstream\n     8→//! sinks.  The design follows the Strategy Pattern so that new enrichment\n     9→//! algorithms can be hot-swapped without recompiling the rest of the\n    10→//! application.\n    11→//\n    12→// Features demonstrated:\n    13→// * Parallel execution of enrichment strategies (tokio tasks / Rayon optional).\n    14→// * Data-quality checks w/ pluggable validation rules.\n    15→// * Scheduling of maintenance tasks (retention, compaction) using cron\n    16→//   patterns.\n    17→// * Observability via [`tracing`] and [`metrics`].\n    18→// * Graceful error handling and back-pressure aware stream processing.\n    19→\n    20→use std::{\n    21→    collections::HashMap,\n    22→    future::Future,\n    23→    pin::Pin,\n    24→    sync::Arc,\n    25→    time::{Duration, SystemTime},\n    26→};\n    27→\n    28→use async_trait::async_trait;\n    29→use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};\n    30→use futures::{stream::FuturesUnordered, Stream, StreamExt};\n    31→use metrics::{counter, gauge, histogram};\n    32→use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\n    33→use tokio::{\n    34→    select,\n    35→    sync::mpsc::{self, Receiver, Sender},\n    36→    task,\n    37→    time,\n    38→};\n    39→use tracing::{debug, error, info, instrument, warn};\n    40→\n    41→/// Maximum number of concurrent events that may be processed.\n    42→///\n    43→/// In practice this would be tuned according to CPU/RAM and downstream\n    44→/// throughput characteristics.\n    45→const MAX_CONCURRENT_EVENTS: usize = 1024;\n    46→\n    47→/// A domain object representing a single unit of social data that entered the\n    48→/// platform.\n    49→///\n    50→/// The struct is intentionally minimal; {\n        let mut current = Some(event);\n        for stage in &self.stages {\n            match current {\n                Some(ev) => match stage.process(ev,).await {\n                    Ok(next) .start_timer();\n\n            // Iterate rules\n            for rule in &self.rules {\n                if let Err(err) = rule.check(&event) {\n                    self.metrics\n .with_label_values(&[\"fail\"])\n                        .observe_duration(timer.stop_and_record());\n\n                    let qe = QualifiedEvent {\n                        original:             .with_label_values(&[\"pass\"])\n                .inc();\n            self.metrics\n                .latency\n                .with_label_values(&[\"pass\"])\n                .observe_duration(timer.stop_and_record());\n\n            let qe = QualifiedEvent {\n                original: event,\n                qc_passed: true,\n                failed_rule: None,\n qc.handle_message(borrowed.clone()).await {\n                        error!(\"QC error: {e}\");\n                    }\n                    // On success *or* failure, we commit offsets.\n                    if let Err(e) = consumer.store_offset_from_message(&borrowed) {\n                        error!(\"Failed to store offset: {e}\");\n                    }\n                }\n                Err(e) => {\n                    error!(\"Kafka error: {e}\");\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    });\n\n    // Listen for SIGINT / SIGTERM\n    tokio::spawn(async move {\n        let This module implements the run-time pluggable enrichment pipeline that\n//! powers ChirpPulse’s real-time social sentiment lake.  A registry of\n//! `EnrichmentStrategy`s can be (un)registered at run-time, enabling data\n//! scientists to hot-swap algorithms without redeploying micro-services.\n//!\n//! The orchestrator asynchronously consumes `SocialEvent`s from an upstream\n//! message queue (Kafka in production, but any `Stream<Item = SocialEvent>`\n//! will do), fans-out processing across registered strategies, merges the\n//! results, and publishes an `EnrichedEvent` downstream.\n//!\n//! Production-grade concerns addressed here:\n//!   • Cancellation safety & graceful shutdown (`shutdown` trigger)\n//!   • Back-pressure via a bounded channel\n//!   • Metrics / structured logging through `tracing`\n//!   • Robust error handling with typed errors\n//!   • Thread-safe shared registry powered by `DashMap`\n//!\n//! NOTE: Networking and external service calls (e.g. to a ML inference\n//!       cluster) are mocked for brevity.\n\nuse std::{\n    sync::Arc,\n    time::{Duration, SystemTime},\n};\n\nuse async_trait::async_trait;\nuse chrono::{DateTime, Utc};\nuse dashmap::DashMap;\nuse futures::{future::join_all, stream::StreamExt};\nuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\nuse thiserror::Error;\nuse tokio::{\n    select,\n    sync::{broadcast, mpsc},\n    task::JoinHandle,\n    time::timeout,\n};\nuse tracing::{debug, error, info, instrument, warn};\n\n/// Maximum time a strategy may take before the orchestrator aborts it.\nconst STRATEGY_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_millis(800);\n\n/// Bounded size for the internal event queue.\nconst CHANNEL_DEPTH: usize = 1_024;\n\n/// Domain object representing a raw social event on the wire.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct SocialEvent {\n    pub id: String,\n    pub author: String,\n    pub text: String,\n    #[serde(with = \"ts_millis\")]\n    pub created_at: SystemTime,\n}\n\n/// Generic enrichment key/value.\npub type Enrichment = serde_json::Map<String, serde_json::Value>;\n\n/// Output object published downstream.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct EnrichedEvent {\n    pub source: SocialEvent,\n    pub enrichments: Enrichment,\n}\n\n/// Custom timestamp (de)serializer for millisecond precision.\nmod ts_millis {\n    use std::time::{Duration, SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};\n\n    use serde::{Deserialize, Deserializer, Serializer};\n\n    pub fn serialize<S>(ts: &SystemTime, s: S) -> Result<S::Ok, S::Error>\n    where\n        S: Serializer,\n    {\n        let millis = ts\n            .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)\n            .unwrap_or(Duration::ZERO)\n            .as_millis() as u64;\n        s.serialize_u64(millis)\n    }\n\n    pub fn deserialize<'de, D>(d: D) -> Result<SystemTime, D::Error>\n    where\n        D: Deserializer<'de>,\n    {\n        let ms = u64::deserialize(d)?;\n        Ok(UNIX_EPOCH + Duration::from_millis(ms))\n    }\n}\n\n/// Failure modes for individual strategies.\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum StrategyError {\n    #[error(\"model inference failed: {0}\")]\n    Inference(String),\n\n    #[error(\"io error: {0}\")]\n    Io(#[from] std::io::Error),\n\n    #[error(\"timeout\")]\n    Timeout,\n\n    #[error(\"unknown: {0}\")]\n    Other(String),\n}\n\n/// Failure modes for the orchestrator itself.\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum OrchestratorError {\n    #[error(transparent)]\n    Strategy(#[from] StrategyError),\n\n    #[error(\"channel closed\")]\n    ChannelClosed,\n\n    #[error(\"shutdown requested\")]\n    Shutdown,\n\n    #[error(\"task join error: {0}\")]\n    Join(#[from] tokio::task::JoinError),\n}\n\n/// Runtime pluggable enrichment strategy.\n///\n/// Implementations are required to be `Send + Sync` so they may be shared\n/// across tokio worker threads.  A real implementation might call out to a\n/// TensorFlow serving cluster, make an HTTP request, or reference an on-disk\n/// model; here we use a toy implementation for illustration.\n#[async_trait]\npub trait EnrichmentStrategy: Send + Sync + 'static {\n    /// Human-readable identifier for logging & registry keys.\n    fn name(&self) -> &'static str;\n\n    /// Attempts to enrich the event, returning a partial `Enrichment`.\n    ///\n    /// The orchestrator will merge the individual strategy outputs into a\n    /// single document.  Returning `Ok(None)` indicates that the strategy\n    /// deliberately produced no output (e.g. unsupported language).\n    async fn enrich(&self, event: &SocialEvent) -> Result<Option<Enrichment>, StrategyError>;\n}\n\n/// Thread-safe registry for run-time strategy hot-swapping.\n#[derive(Debug, Default)]\npub struct StrategyRegistry {\n    strategies: DashMap<String, Arc<dyn EnrichmentStrategy>>,\n}\n\nimpl StrategyRegistry {\n    pub fn new() -> Self {\n        Self::default()\n    }\n\n    /// Registers or replaces a strategy.\n    pub fn register<S>(&self, strategy: S)\n    where\n        S: EnrichmentStrategy,\n    {\n        let key = strategy.name().to_lowercase();\n        self.strategies.insert(key, Arc::new(strategy));\n        info!(strategy = %key, \"strategy registered\");\n    }\n\n    /// Removes a strategy from the registry.\n    pub fn unregister(&self, name: &str) {\n        self.strategies.remove(name);\n        info!(strategy = %name, \"strategy unregistered\");\n    }\n\n    /// Snapshot of the current strategies.\n    pub fn snapshot(&self) -> Vec<Arc<dyn EnrichmentStrategy>> {\n        self.strategies.iter().map(|s| Arc::clone(s.value())).collect()\n    }\n}\n\n/// Orchestrates ingestion, fan-out enrichment, and publication.\npub struct EnrichmentOrchestrator {\n    input_rx: mpsc::Receiver<SocialEvent>,\n    output_tx: mpsc::Sender<EnrichedEvent>,\n    registry: StrategyRegistry,\n    shutdown_rx: broadcast::Receiver<()>,\n}\n\n/// Builder for `EnrichmentOrchestrator`.\npub struct OrchestratorBuilder {\n    registry: StrategyRegistry,\n    shutdown_rx: broadcast::Receiver<()>,\n}\n\nimpl OrchestratorBuilder {\n    pub fn new(shutdown_rx: broadcast::Receiver<()>) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            registry: StrategyRegistry::new(),\n            shutdown_rx,\n        }\n    }\n\n    pub fn with_strategy<S>(mut self, strategy: S) -> Self\n    where\n        S: EnrichmentStrategy,\n    {\n        self.registry.register(strategy);\n        self\n    }\n\n    pub fn build(\n        self,\n    ) -> (\n        EnrichmentOrchestrator,\n        mpsc::Sender<SocialEvent>,\n        mpsc::Receiver<EnrichedEvent>,\n    ) {\n        let (in_tx, in_rx) = mpsc::channel(CHANNEL_DEPTH);\n        let (out_tx, out_rx) = mpsc::channel(CHANNEL_DEPTH);\n        (\n            EnrichmentOrchestrator {\n                input_rx: in_rx,\n                output_tx: out_tx,\n                registry: self.registry,\n                shutdown_rx: self.shutdown_rx,\n            },\n            in_tx,\n            out_rx,\n        )\n    }\n}\n\nimpl EnrichmentOrchestrator {\n    /// Starts the main processing loop.  Returns when shutdown is requested\n    /// or an unrecoverable error occurs.\n    #[instrument(skip(self))]\n    pub async fn run(mut self) -> Result<(), OrchestratorError> {\n        loop {\n            select! {\n                _ = self.shutdown_rx.recv() => {\n                    info!(\"shutdown signal received\");\n                    return Err(OrchestratorError::Shutdown);\n                }\n                maybe_event = self.input_rx.recv() => {\n                    let event = maybe_event.ok_or(OrchestratorError::ChannelClosed)?;\n                    debug!(event_id = %event.id, \"received event\");\n                    let record = self.handle_event(event).await?;\n                    self.output_tx\n                        .send(record)\n                        .await\n                        .map_err(|_| OrchestratorError::ChannelClosed)?;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[instrument(skip(self, event))]\n    async fn handle_event(&self, event: SocialEvent) -> Result<EnrichedEvent, OrchestratorError> {\n        let strategies = self.registry.snapshot();\n        let mut tasks: Vec<JoinHandle<_>> = Vec::with_capacity(strategies.len());\n\n        for strat in strategies {\n            let e = event.clone();\n            tasks.push(tokio::spawn(async move {\n                let name = strat.name();\n                let result = timeout(STRATEGY_TIMEOUT, strat.enrich(&e)).await;\n                match result {\n                    Ok(Ok(enrichment)) => Ok((name, enrichment)),\n                    Ok(Err(e)) => Err((name, StrategyError::from(e))),\n                    Err(_) => Err((name, StrategyError::Timeout)),\n                }\n            }));\n        }\n\n        let mut merged = Enrichment::new();\n\n        for handle in tasks {\n            match handle.await? {\n                Ok((name, Some(partial))) => {\n                    debug!(strategy = %name, \"enrichment succeeded\");\n                    merged.extend(partial);\n                }\n                Ok((name, None)) => {\n                    debug!(strategy = %name, \"no enrichment produced\");\n                }\n                Err((name, err)) => {\n                    warn!(strategy = %name, %err, \"enrichment failed\");\n                    // Continue processing; per-strategy failures are tolerated.\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        Ok(EnrichedEvent {\n            source: event,\n            enrichments: merged,\n        })\n    }\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Built-in demo strategies                                                  */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/// Toy sentiment analysis strategy using naive rules.\npub struct NaiveSentiment;\n\n#[async_trait]\nimpl EnrichmentStrategy for NaiveSentiment {\n    fn name(&self) -> &'static str {\n        \"naive_sentiment\"\n    }\n\n    async fn enrich(&self, event: &SocialEvent) -> Result<Option<Enrichment>, StrategyError> {\n        // Simulate heavy computation.\n        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(50)).await;\n\n        let score = if event.text.contains(\"love\") {\n            1.0\n        } else if event.text.contains(\"hate\") {\n            -1.0\n        } else {\n            0.0\n        };\n\n        let mut map = Enrichment::new();\n        map.insert(\"sentiment_score\".into(), serde_json::json!(score));\n        Ok(Some(map))\n    }\n}\n\n/// Toy language detector based on the presence of unicode ranges.\npub struct SimpleLangDetect;\n\n#[async_trait]\nimpl EnrichmentStrategy for SimpleLangDetect {\n    fn name(&self) -> &'static str {\n        \"simple_lang_detect\"\n    }\n\n    async fn enrich(&self, event: &SocialEvent) -> Result<Option<Enrichment>, StrategyError> {\n        use unicode_script::{Script, UnicodeScript};\n\n        let mut counts = std::collections::HashMap::<Script, usize>::new();\n        for ch in event.text.chars() {\n            *counts.entry(ch.script()).or_default() += 1;\n        }\n\n        let lang = if counts.keys().any(|s| *s == Script::Cyrillic) {\n            \"ru\"\n        } else if counts.keys().any(|s| *s == Script::Han) {\n            \"zh\"\n        } else {\n            \"en\"\n        };\n\n        let mut map = Enrichment::new();\n        map.insert(\"language\".into(), serde_json::json!(lang));\n        Ok(Some(map))\n    }\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Example Entrypoint (could be removed in library context)                  */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use tokio::sync::broadcast;\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn integration_flow_works() {\n        tracing_subscriber::fmt::try_init().ok();\n\n        let (shutdown_tx, shutdown_rx) = broadcast::channel(1);\n        let builder = OrchestratorBuilder::new(shutdown_rx)\n            .with_strategy(NaiveSentiment)\n            .with_strategy(SimpleLangDetect);\n        let (orch, in_tx, mut out_rx) = builder.build();\n\n        let orch_handle = tokio::spawn(async move { orch.run().await });\n\n        // Simulate producer\n        in_tx\n            .send(SocialEvent {\n                id: \"42\".into(),\n                author: \"alice\".into(),\n                text: \"I love Rust 🦀!\".into(),\n                created_at: SystemTime::now(),\n            })\n            .await\n            .unwrap();\n\n        // Verify output\n        let enriched = out_rx.recv().await.unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(enriched.source.id, \"42\");\n        assert!(enriched.enrichments.contains_key(\"sentiment_score\"));\n        assert!(enriched.enrichments.contains_key(\"language\"));\n\n        // Shutdown orchestrator\n        shutdown_tx.send(()).unwrap();\n        orch_handle.await.unwrap().unwrap_err(); // Expect Shutdown\n    }\n}\n```","numLines":403,"startLine":1,"totalLines":403}}}

Dynamic enrichment pipeline with hot-swappable strategies.\n//! This module represents a self-contained “micro-pipeline” that can be embedded\n//! in any streaming consumer (e.g., a Kafka consumer task).
